- Delegated the coverage docs refresh for this expression-planning slice to
  `gpt-5.3-codex-spark`, reviewed the generated diff, and restored the
  surrounding `docs/testing.md` coverage explanation after the scripted block
  replacement. Workspace line coverage is now `83.99%`.
- Added `GltfMaterialTextureSlots::binding_plan`, a renderer-neutral MToon
  texture binding plan that attaches each shader slot to its texture index,
  sRGB/linear upload table, and white/black/neutral-normal fallback. The wgpu
  and Bevy render captures now consume this shared plan instead of duplicating
  slot-specific fallback policy at the renderer edge.
- Added `GltfMaterialUvTransforms::uniform_plan` so shader-facing UV transform
  packing is renderer-neutral. The shared `GltfMaterialUvUniformPlan` now
  resolves offset/scale defaults, rotation values, UV animation scroll/rotation,
  and the current texCoord0-only policy before wgpu and Bevy convert it into
  backend uniform types.
- Centralized renderer-facing material shading input selection in
  `LoadedVrm::material_shading_plan`. The shared plan now chooses MToon versus
  glTF/PBR fallback values, effective emissive strength, normal scale, rim,
  matcap, metallic/roughness, unlit, and VRM0 compatibility flags before the
  concrete wgpu and Bevy captures apply runtime expression color overrides.
- Moved the capture-side MToon light accumulator policy into
  `vrm-adapter::MtoonLightingConfig`. The shared helper now resolves the tuned
  diagnostic values and the reference-shaped `three-vrm` accumulator into the
  `[exposure, ambient_base, ambient_gi_scale, pbr_ambient]` uniform payload used
  by both concrete wgpu and Bevy render captures.
- Moved CPU-side `KHR_texture_transform` application for texCoord0 into
  `vrm-io::transform_tex_coord_0`. The helper preserves the existing behavior
  of ignoring transforms targeting non-zero UV sets while sharing offset,
  scale, and rotation math between the concrete wgpu and Bevy capture outline
  texture sampling paths.
- Centralized renderer-facing material UV transform resolution in
  `LoadedVrm::material_uv_transforms`. The shared `GltfMaterialUvTransforms`
  now resolves MToon transforms, glTF base/normal/emissive/occlusion fallbacks,
  shade fallback-to-base behavior, and MToon UV animation scroll/rotation at a
  requested time. The wgpu and Bevy captures still apply expression-driven
  texture transform overrides locally, but the base material transform plan now
  comes from the same Sans I/O layer.
- Centralized renderer-facing material texture slot resolution in
  `LoadedVrm::material_texture_slots`. The shared `GltfMaterialTextureSlots`
  now resolves MToon texture slots, glTF base/normal fallbacks, emissive and
  occlusion textures, and outline/UV-animation mask slots after validating that
  the texture index exists. The concrete wgpu and Bevy captures now consume the
  same slot table, reducing another source of render-parity drift for
  wgpu/Bevy/ash/custom integrations.
- Moved CPU-side RGBA texture sampling for render capture outline-width
  textures into `vrm-io::CpuRgba8Image`. The helper now owns repeat/linear
  channel sampling and makes the vertical sampling origin explicit through
  `Rgba8SamplingOrigin`, preserving the current wgpu and Bevy capture behavior
  while removing another renderer-edge duplicate. This keeps texture sampling
  diagnostics usable for ash/custom renderer parity work without depending on a
  GPU backend.
- Moved capture image-format normalization out of the concrete wgpu/Bevy
  examples into `vrm-io::image_data_to_rgba8` and
  `vrm-io::image_bytes_to_rgba8`. Texture upload planning now has a Sans I/O
  path for both source image byte normalization and shared box mip-chain
  generation, so wgpu/Bevy/ash/custom renderers can share byte-for-byte input
  preparation before touching engine texture APIs. `vrm-io` unit tests cover
  R8/RG/RGB/RGBA conversion, invalid lengths, zero dimensions, and unsupported
  16/float source formats.
- Moved capture mip-chain generation out of the concrete wgpu/Bevy examples
  into `vrm-io::generate_rgba_mip_chain`, a renderer-neutral Sans I/O helper
  that validates dimensions and returns explicit `RgbaMipLevel` values. Both
  render captures now consume the same shared box mip chain, reducing the risk
  that future wgpu/Bevy/ash parity work drifts by backend. `vrm-io` unit tests
  cover the non-square mip sequence and invalid RGBA input errors.
- Updated the local Rust CI/render runner to launch cargo commands with dev/test
  debug info level `1`. The Bevy-heavy render capture and coverage builds were
  repeatedly hitting MSVC PDB limits (`LNK1318` / `LNK1140`) even after source
  checks passed; keeping line-level debug info preserves the local gate and
  coverage workflow without changing render behavior.
- Added renderer-facing `KHR_materials_unlit` extraction for glTF materials and
  wired it into the concrete wgpu/Bevy PBR fallback paths. The MToon branch
  intentionally keeps ignoring glTF unlit when `VRMC_materials_mtoon` is
  present, matching the measured three-vrm behavior on the official UV
  animation sample where the VRMC MToon material path wins over the glTF unlit
  extension. The capture texture mip-chain generator now uses CatmullRom
  downsampling, which better matches the WebGL/three-vrm generated-mipmap
  reference for the current fixture set. The six-fixture `rgb-visible` sweep is
  now Seed-san wgpu `34.6181 dB` / Bevy `34.0835 dB`, constraint wgpu
  `36.2443 dB` / Bevy `36.2352 dB`, UV animation wgpu `35.5223 dB` / Bevy
  `35.5023 dB`, expression masks around `55.2-55.7 dB`, and Alicia VRM0 wgpu
  `35.6238 dB` / Bevy `35.6088 dB`. The object-body `rgb-nonblack` floor rose
  to UV animation Bevy `26.9834 dB`, so `just render-parity-samples-nonblack`
  now enforces `26.9 dB`.
- Replaced the wgpu capture's material-wide sampler approximation with
  per-texture-slot sampler bindings for base, shade, shading shift, matcap,
  rim, normal, emissive, UV animation mask, and occlusion textures. The shader
  now samples each slot through the sampler attached to that glTF texture,
  matching the Bevy path's per-image sampler descriptors more closely. The
  generated embedded PNG IO test now covers two glTF textures sharing one image
  while carrying different sampler policies, keeping texture-index preservation
  source-like and regression-tested.
- Added object-body PSNR diagnostics for opaque-black render parity sweeps.
  `compare-psnr.mjs` and `tools/ci/local-ci.rs` now accept `rgb-nonblack` and
  `rgb-nonblack-interior1px`, and `just render-parity-samples-nonblack` runs
  the same six real fixtures with selected `rgb-nonblack >= 26.9 dB`. That
  sweep passes with exact alpha parity after the capture paths started honoring
  glTF sampler min/mag/wrap policy per texture; current selected PSNR is
  Seed-san wgpu `27.5383 dB` / Bevy `27.0036 dB`, constraint wgpu
  `28.2818 dB` / Bevy `28.2728 dB`, UV animation wgpu `27.0034 dB` / Bevy
  `26.9834 dB`, expression masks around `45.7-46.2 dB`, and Alicia VRM0 wgpu
  `28.1749 dB` / Bevy `28.1599 dB`. The current object-body floor is now the
  UV animation sample rather than Alicia, and remaining work should raise
  actual model-body color parity beyond the current `26.9 dB` regression floor.
- Added structured glTF sampler extraction to `vrm-io` (`GltfSamplerData`,
  min/mag filters, and wrap modes) and made the concrete wgpu/Bevy capture
  examples consume it at the renderer edge. This fixes the previous global
  sampler approximation: Seed-san and the constraint sample keep their
  `LINEAR_MIPMAP_NEAREST` behavior, while Alicia's VRM0 `LINEAR` samplers stop
  using unwanted mip levels. The six-fixture `just render-parity-samples` gate
  now enforces selected `rgb-visible >= 34 dB`; current selected PSNR is
  Seed-san wgpu `34.5645 dB` / Bevy `34.0434 dB`, constraint wgpu
  `36.2028 dB` / Bevy `36.1877 dB`, UV animation wgpu `35.2517 dB` / Bevy
  `35.2073 dB`, expression masks around `55.2-55.7 dB`, and Alicia VRM0 wgpu
  `35.6238 dB` / Bevy `35.6088 dB`.
- Aligned concrete capture defaults more closely with three-vrm source policy:
  VRM0 compatibility shade clamping is now an explicit capture flag instead of
  being forced for every VRM0 compat asset, matching three-vrm's default
  `v0CompatShade = false`; MToon matcap UV calculation now uses view-space
  direction/normal in the wgpu and Bevy capture shaders. On the fixed Alicia
  camera these correctness changes did not materially change PSNR, which keeps
  the remaining blocker focused on broader material/geometry color deltas.
- Tightened the real render-parity gates with a split threshold. A trial `34 dB`
  floor for the full six-fixture `just render-parity-samples` sweep showed
  Alicia VRM0 compatibility is the current lower bound at wgpu `32.6863 dB` /
  Bevy `32.6757 dB`, so the full sweep now enforces selected
  `rgb-visible >= 32.5 dB` while still keeping Alicia in the default sample set.
  Added `just render-parity-vrm1-samples` for the same Seed-san, constraint, UV
  animation, and binary-expression fixtures without Alicia; that VRM1/current
  official subset passes `rgb-visible >= 34 dB` with exact opaque alpha parity.
- Added a public renderer primitive material policy layer in `vrm-adapter`. `RendererMaterialPipelinePlan` now derives alpha/cull/depth/blend/render-order/phase-order state from `MtoonRendererMaterialPlan`, and `GltfMaterialPipelineOverride` applies glTF alpha cutoff and double-sided overrides as a pure adapter-layer transition. The wgpu/Bevy capture examples now use this public plan via the shared `CaptureMaterialPlan` alias, and the wgpu/ash material skeleton maps the same primitive plan into concrete engine pipeline keys.
- Promoted renderer-owned MToon material planning into `vrm-adapter`. `mtoon_renderer_material_plans` now exposes `MtoonRendererMaterialPlan`, pipeline state, shader parameters, texture refs, texture-slot bindings, and sampler hints for non-Bevy engines; `vrm-adapter-bevy` and `examples/mtoon_renderer_skeletons.rs` now consume that public plan instead of re-deriving the same state from raw `MtoonPipelinePass` values. Focused `vrm-adapter` coverage, `cargo run --example mtoon_renderer_skeletons`, and `cargo run --example bevy_mtoon_materialization` pass. The coverage snapshot refresh was delegated to `gpt-5.3-codex-spark` and reviewed; workspace line coverage is now `82.96%`.
- Shared the concrete capture material policy through a backend-neutral `CaptureMaterialPlan` in `examples/common/render_capture_scene.rs`. The wgpu and Bevy render captures now consume the same MToon/glTF alpha, cull, depth-write, blend, render-order, and transparent queue decisions before converting to backend-specific pipeline types. `just render-parity-transparent-queue-matrix D:/git/three-vrm` still passes after the refactor with identical alpha buckets, selected `rgb-visible` PSNR wgpu `53.0342 dB` / Bevy `48.4839 dB`, and max selected channel delta `2` / `3`.
- Refactored the local render-parity runner toward a Sans I/O boundary. `tools/ci/local-ci.rs` now keeps filesystem operations in thin wrappers while RGBA JSON parsing, diff-heatmap generation, PSNR report summary extraction, and summary Markdown formatting operate on in-memory strings, JSON values, or `RgbaArtifact` buffers. `just render-parity-transparent-queue-matrix D:/git/three-vrm` still passes after the refactor, proving PNG/diff/summary artifact generation remains wired through the new pure helpers.

## 2026-05-30

- Added a broader transparent queue-matrix render-parity guard. `tools/render-parity/generate-transparent-fixture.rs --case queue-matrix` now emits `.external-fixtures/generated/transparent-queue-matrix.vrm.gltf` with overlapping MToon `BLEND` layers that combine texture alpha, non-identity `KHR_texture_transform`, mixed render queue offsets, `transparentWithZWrite`, forced shade, parametric rim, and emissive texture strength. `just render-parity-transparent-queue-matrix` uses the exact `three-vrm` accumulator on a transparent background and passes with identical alpha buckets (`transparent=512`, `opaque=0`, `partial=65024`), only 1-LSB alpha rounding, selected `rgb-visible` PSNR wgpu `53.0342 dB` / Bevy `48.4839 dB`, and max selected channel delta `2` / `3`.
- Aligned concrete wgpu/Bevy outline expansion with the current three-vrm WebGL vertex shader by applying the local/object normal offset after morph/skin position deformation instead of switching to a skinned-normal offset direction. The focused real normal-map/constraint sweep remains numerically unchanged (Seed-san wgpu `34.5645 dB` / Bevy `34.0434 dB`, constraint wgpu `36.2028 dB` / Bevy `36.1877 dB`), which confirms the remaining constraint outline residual is not explained by that pre/post-skin normal-direction branch. The local render summary parser now also accepts `"Infinity"` PSNR strings from `compare-psnr.mjs`, so `just render-parity-screen-outline-generated` completes again and reports wgpu `Infinity` / Bevy `53.2689 dB` on selected `rgb-opaque` with the known `188` alpha-edge mismatches.
- Broadened the exact MToon texture-slot/light-color guard with `shadeMultiplyTexture`. `tools/render-parity/generate-mtoon-texture-fixture.rs` now adds a generated shade-texture swatch before the existing shading-shift, rim, UV-animation-mask, and outline-width swatches. `just render-parity-mtoon-textures-generated` now also enforces selected-channel delta `<= 8` and exact alpha; the current run reports selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR wgpu `53.8749 dB` / Bevy `51.2935 dB`, max selected channel delta `8`, and alpha mismatches `0`.
- Added a lighted transparent-material render-parity guard. `tools/render-parity/generate-transparent-fixture.rs --case lighted` now emits `.external-fixtures/generated/transparent-lighted.vrm.gltf` with overlapping MToon `BLEND` layers that combine texture alpha, `transparentWithZWrite`, direct/shade lighting, parametric rim, and emissive texture plus `KHR_materials_emissive_strength` before transparent blending. `just render-parity-transparent-lighted` uses the exact `three-vrm` accumulator on a transparent background and passes with identical alpha buckets (`transparent=512`, `opaque=0`, `partial=65024`), selected `rgb-visible` PSNR wgpu `53.7519 dB` / Bevy `50.2049 dB`, max selected channel delta `2` / `3`, and alpha max delta `2`.
- Strengthened normal-gate VRMA application coverage in `vrm-adapter`. The external three-vrm golden tests still cover real `test.vrma` and `idle_loop.vrma` parity.
- Added non-rendering materialization example smokes to the local Rust CI gate. The same Markdown is embedded at the top of `visual-review.html`, so PSNR and visual review evidence stay in one artifact set for broader three-vrm compatibility audits.

- Expanded the exact MToon light/color generated fixture from 10 to 12 swatches by adding `toon-ramp-mid-normal` and `toon-ramp-shifted-mid`, so the guard now covers linearstep mid-ramp interpolation in addition to forced lit/shade extremes. `just render-parity-mtoon-light-generated`, direct-only, colored-direct, ambient-only, and scaled-colored recipes all pass with the expanded swatch set. Current selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR is default wgpu `59.7573 dB` / Bevy `51.1653 dB`, direct-only wgpu `59.7409 dB` / Bevy `51.6508 dB`, colored-direct wgpu `59.5993 dB` / Bevy `51.9037 dB`, ambient-only wgpu `61.7386 dB` / Bevy `54.5071 dB`, and scaled-colored wgpu `61.9831 dB` / Bevy `51.6960 dB`, all with max selected channel delta `<= 2`.
- Extended the exact MToon light/color generated guard across default, direct-only, and ambient-only light setups. `just render-parity-mtoon-light-generated`, `just render-parity-mtoon-light-direct-generated`, and `just render-parity-mtoon-light-ambient-generated` now all enforce aggregate selected-channel deltas (`<= 2`) and per-swatch color reports. The swatch guard uses max channel delta `<= 2`, with per-swatch PSNR floors, so direct, indirect, rim/matcap, shade, and emissive drift are caught separately instead of being hidden by aggregate PSNR.
- Added a normal-map-off real-fixture diagnostic. `tools/render-parity/three-vrm-browser-capture.mjs`, `examples/wgpu_render_capture.rs`, `examples/bevy_render_capture.rs`, and `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-parity` now accept normal-map disabling, exposed by `just render-parity-normal-maps-off`. With normal maps disabled across all three renderers, Seed-san improves to wgpu `33.5967 dB` / Bevy `33.1992 dB`, while the constraint sample stays roughly flat at wgpu `35.9609 dB` / Bevy `35.9543 dB`. This confirms Seed-san's remaining delta includes normal-map/tangentless behavior, while the constraint residual is more outline/geometry-side.
- Added an opt-in shader-derivative tangent-frame diagnostic for tangentless normal maps. `examples/wgpu_render_capture.rs`, `examples/bevy_render_capture.rs`, and `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-parity` now accept `--normal-map-mode derivative` / `--render-normal-map-mode derivative`, exposed by `just render-parity-normal-maps-derivative`. The corrected derivative path compiles and preserves exact alpha parity, but it is still worse than the default generated-tangent path on real fixtures: Seed-san wgpu `30.8717 dB` / Bevy `30.6436 dB`, constraint sample wgpu `35.9214 dB` / Bevy `35.9149 dB`. The default remains generated tangents until a closer three-vrm fallback beats the focused real normal-map guard.
- Tightened generated transparent-material render parity from PSNR-only floors to PSNR plus worst-case error gates. `tools/render-parity/compare-psnr.mjs` now accepts `--max-selected-channel-delta` and `--max-alpha-delta`, and `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-parity` forwards them through `--render-max-selected-channel-delta` / `--render-max-alpha-delta`. The transparent generated, high-contrast, broad, alpha-mode, and depth-stack `just` recipes now enforce bounded RGB/alpha deltas in addition to selected PSNR; the focused transparent runs all pass with current thresholds (`49/51/48/47/49 dB` respectively).
- Added an exact-light-units guard for MToon light/color accumulation. `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-sync-three-vrm-light-units` now derives the Rust capture `direct-light-scale` from three-vrm's `DirectionalLight.intensity / PI` and `pbr-ambient` from three-vrm's `AmbientLight.intensity / PI`, removing manual drift in reference-shaped lighting recipes. `just render-parity-mtoon-light-scaled-colored-generated` exercises non-default intensity (`2.3561945`), non-default ambient (`0.25`), and non-white light color (`0.35, 0.72, 1.0`); it passes selected `rgb-interior1px` at wgpu `61.2438 dB` / Bevy `51.4998 dB`, with max selected channel delta `1` / `2` and per-swatch max deltas within the same bounds.
- Added an outline-off render parity diagnostic. The three-vrm browser capture plus wgpu and Bevy examples now accept outline disabling, and `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-parity` exposes it as `--render-disable-outlines`; `just render-parity-outline-off` writes `.external-fixtures/render-parity-outline-off/`. The diagnostic passes exact alpha parity. Seed-san remains essentially flat at wgpu `32.5179 dB` / Bevy `32.5257 dB`, while the constraint sample improves to wgpu `36.8185 dB` / Bevy `36.8061 dB`, confirming outline expansion is a measurable blocker for constraint but not the main Seed-san delta.
- Added a focused real normal-map render parity recipe, `just render-parity-real-normal-maps`, for the unique official/local MToon normal-map fixtures whose primitives omit glTF `TANGENT`. It writes `.external-fixtures/render-parity-real-normal-maps/` and currently covers Seed-san (`6` normal-mapped primitives without tangents) plus `VRM1_Constraint_Twist_Sample.vrm` (`3`). The run passes exact alpha parity and selected `rgb-visible >= 32 dB`: Seed-san wgpu `32.8751 dB` / Bevy `32.5273 dB`, and constraint sample wgpu `35.9456 dB` / Bevy `35.9394 dB`.
- Raised the focused real tangentless normal-map fixture floor from `32 dB` to `32.5 dB` after re-running `just render-parity-real-normal-maps`. The guard still covers Seed-san (`6` normal-mapped primitives without glTF `TANGENT`) and `VRM1_Constraint_Twist_Sample.vrm` (`3`) with exact alpha parity. Current selected `rgb-visible` PSNR remains Seed-san wgpu `32.8751 dB` / Bevy `32.5273 dB`, and constraint sample wgpu `35.9456 dB` / Bevy `35.9394 dB`; the new floor is intentionally just below the Seed-san Bevy result so future regressions in real tangentless normal-map handling are caught earlier.
- Raised the opt-in generated MToon normal-map render parity floor after the Bevy generated tangent fix. `just render-parity-samples` still passes the six real-fixture opaque sweep with zero alpha mismatches; the latest selected `rgb-visible` PSNR is Seed-san wgpu `32.8751 dB` / Bevy `32.5273 dB`, constraint sample wgpu `35.9456 dB` / Bevy `35.9394 dB`, UV animation wgpu `34.8985 dB` / Bevy `34.8819 dB`, expression masks around `39.29-39.30 dB`, and Alicia VRM0 wgpu `32.3424 dB` / Bevy `32.3410 dB`. `just render-parity-mtoon-normal-generated` now enforces `rgb-interior1px >= 45 dB` instead of the old `38 dB` floor.
- Closed the Bevy generated-normal regression in the opt-in MToon normal-map fixture. The Bevy capture path now preserves generated tangent frames even when a primitive's accessor contains unreferenced vertices, and the custom material specialization explicitly enables `VERTEX_TANGENTS` for tangent-carrying meshes. `just render-parity-mtoon-normal-generated` still passes exact alpha parity and now reports selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR wgpu `47.1013 dB` / Bevy `46.0637 dB`, reducing Bevy's max selected channel delta from `41` to `13`.
- Added renderer-side direct-light scaling for exact MToon light/color audits. `examples/wgpu_render_capture.rs`, `examples/bevy_render_capture.rs`, and `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-parity` now accept `--direct-light-scale` / `--render-direct-light-scale`, so Rust captures can match three-vrm reference runs where `DirectionalLight` intensity is not `Math.PI`. A new `just render-parity-mtoon-light-ambient-generated` recipe disables directional light on both sides while keeping ambient `0.1`; the generated MToon light/color fixture passes with selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR wgpu `60.9964 dB` and Bevy `53.7214 dB`, max selected channel deltas `1` and `2`, and the known edge-only alpha mismatch `330/512`.
- Added a source-like transparent depth-stack render parity fixture. `tools/render-parity/generate-transparent-depth-fixture.rs` writes `.external-fixtures/generated/transparent-depth-stack.vrm.gltf` with three same-render-order MToon `BLEND` layers at different depths, including one texture-alpha layer, so the renderer harness now covers depth-sensitive transparent accumulation in addition to same-plane queue ordering. `just render-parity-transparent-depth-stack` renders it on a transparent background with exact alpha-bucket parity and a 1-LSB alpha channel tolerance; the current run reports identical alpha buckets (`transparent=31672`, `opaque=0`, `partial=33864`), no alpha deltas beyond 1, selected `rgb-visible` PSNR wgpu `49.9331 dB`, and Bevy `51.8518 dB`.
- Added configurable three-vrm reference light parameters to the browser capture and local Rust CI render runner. `tools/render-parity/three-vrm-browser-capture.mjs` now accepts directional intensity/vector and ambient intensity, and `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-parity` forwards `--render-three-vrm-*` options. A new `just render-parity-mtoon-light-direct-generated` recipe disables ambient on both sides (`three-vrm AmbientLight=0`, Rust `pbrAmbient=0`) while keeping `DirectionalLight(Math.PI)`. The direct-only generated MToon light/color run passes with selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR wgpu `58.9803 dB` and Bevy `51.7674 dB`, max selected channel deltas `1` and `2`, and the same edge-only alpha mismatch `330/512`.
- Broadened the generated MToon light/color fixture with two emission swatches: one pure `emissiveFactor` material and one `emissiveTexture` plus `KHR_materials_emissive_strength` material. `just render-parity-mtoon-light-generated` still passes the `50 dB` interior-color floor with exact emission accumulation included: selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR is wgpu `58.9968 dB` with max selected channel delta `1`, and Bevy `51.8032 dB` with max selected channel delta `2`; the known edge-only alpha mismatch is now `330/512`. The three-vrm browser reference artifact also now records renderer/light/camera metadata (`THREE.REVISION`, `outputColorSpace`, `toneMapping`, `DirectionalLight(Math.PI)`, ambient intensity, and camera frustum) under `reference`, so PSNR reports can be tied back to the exact scene-light conditions that produced the reference image.
- Added an opt-in generated MToon normal-map parity fixture. `tools/render-parity/generate-mtoon-texture-fixture.rs --include-normal` now writes `.external-fixtures/generated/mtoon-normal-texture.vrm.gltf`, and `just render-parity-mtoon-normal-generated` compares it against three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy with exact alpha buckets/mismatches (`0`). The normal-map swatch deliberately omits glTF `TANGENT` because a synthetic tangent attribute hit a three-vrm WebGL `vTangent` varying validation failure; this keeps the fixture focused on three-vrm's tangentless fallback behavior. Flipping tangent-space normal Y in the wgpu/Bevy capture shaders improved the generated normal fixture's wgpu selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR from `36.4340 dB` to `47.1013 dB`; Bevy currently passes the lower guard at `38.4018 dB` with max selected channel delta `41`. The broader official opaque and transparent sweeps still pass after this shader change.
- Added a source-like transparent alpha-mode fixture and fixed the wgpu capture path to honor glTF/MToon alpha cutoff instead of using a hard-coded `0.5` discard threshold. `tools/render-parity/generate-transparent-alpha-modes-fixture.rs` writes `.external-fixtures/generated/transparent-alpha-modes.vrm.gltf` with separated OPAQUE, MASK-pass, MASK-fail, and BLEND swatches. `just render-parity-transparent-alpha-modes` now compares three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy on a transparent background with exact alpha buckets (`transparent=37904`, `opaque=19184`, `partial=8448`) and zero alpha mismatches. The current selected `rgb-visible` PSNR is wgpu `47.4970 dB` and Bevy `48.0126 dB`, both with max selected channel delta `2`.
- Extended `inspect-mtoon-fixtures.rs` to count normal-mapped primitives by TANGENT availability. The official fixture inventory now shows that every current normal-mapped official primitive lacks glTF `TANGENT`: Seed-san has `6/0/6`, each constraint sample copy has `3/0/3`, and the official total is `21` normal-mapped primitives without tangents. This makes normal-map parity a concrete next renderer target rather than a vague material-breadth bucket. I also tested a direct derivative-tangent fallback in the wgpu/Bevy capture path, but rejected that implementation because the first Seed-san run dropped to `20.3102 dB`; the current committed render path remains the previous CPU-generated tangent path until a closer three-vrm derivative formulation is implemented and measured.
- Added a source-like MToon texture-slot render parity fixture, `tools/render-parity/generate-mtoon-texture-fixture.rs`, plus `just render-parity-mtoon-textures-generated`. It exercises `shadingShiftTexture`, `rimMultiplyTexture`, `uvAnimationMaskTexture` at `mtoon-time=1.0`, and `outlineWidthMultiplyTexture` without committing binary sample assets. The current valid run passes with exact alpha buckets/mismatches (`0`) and selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR wgpu `53.5556` and Bevy `50.9425`; the recipe enforces a `50 dB` floor. Normal-map breadth now lives in the separate opt-in `render-parity-mtoon-normal-generated` fixture above so the high-signal texture-slot guard can keep its `50 dB` floor.
- Added a local MToon fixture feature scanner, `tools/render-parity/inspect-mtoon-fixtures.rs`, plus `just inspect-mtoon-fixtures`, so material/outline/transparent coverage can be audited from local fixtures without committing binary samples. The current official fixture inventory reports `74` MToon materials, `0` screen-coordinate outline materials, `4` transparent-ZWrite materials, `3` UV-animation materials, `13` normal-textured materials, and `11` matcap-textured materials. The generated fixture inventory confirms the source-like guard set adds one screen-coordinate outline fixture and three transparent-ZWrite cases. Because no current official fixture contains `outlineWidthMode = screenCoordinates`, `just render-parity-screen-outline-generated` remains the authoritative screen-outline parity gate; the latest run still passes with selected `rgb-opaque` PSNR wgpu `Infinity` and Bevy `53.2689`, alpha mismatches `188/256`.
- Corrected the concrete wgpu/Bevy MToon capture light vector after the expanded generated MToon light fixture exposed that the previous vector only matched three-vrm for front-facing normals. The Rust render paths now transform the three-vrm `DirectionalLight(1,1,1)` convention through the same VRM orientation compensation, using `(-1, 1, -1)` in capture space. The generated MToon light fixture now contains eight visible swatches, including two low-toony ramp materials with angled normals; `just render-parity-mtoon-light-generated` passes with selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR wgpu `58.9193` and Bevy `52.3057`. The exact `three-vrm` accumulator sweep now records Seed-san wgpu `33.7678` / Bevy `33.3385` and constraint wgpu `36.1974` / Bevy `36.1825`. The six-fixture opaque sweep improved materially: selected `rgb-visible` is now Seed-san wgpu `32.3546` / Bevy `32.0485`, constraint wgpu `35.9421` / Bevy `35.9362`, UV animation wgpu `34.8985` / Bevy `34.8819`, expression masks around `39.29-39.30`, and Alicia VRM0 wgpu `32.3424` / Bevy `32.3410`; `just render-parity-samples` and `just render-parity-real-transparent` now enforce a `32 dB` floor.
- Broadened the license-safe transparent material parity fixture. `generate-transparent-fixture.rs --case broad` now emits four overlapping MToon `BLEND` primitives with mixed render queue offsets, `transparentWithZWrite`, a high-contrast palette, and a PNG base-color texture with per-texel alpha. `just render-parity-transparent-broad` compares three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy on a transparent background with exact alpha-bucket parity and only a 1-LSB alpha channel tolerance for GPU/browser rounding; the current run reports identical alpha buckets (`transparent=512`, `opaque=0`, `partial=65024`), no alpha deltas beyond 1, selected `rgb-visible` PSNR wgpu `48.5282` and Bevy `48.5944`. The PSNR report now records alpha `maxDelta` and `mismatchesBeyondOne`, and the local CI runner exposes `--render-alpha-channel-tolerance`.
- Added glTF `occlusionTexture` extraction to `vrm-io` and carried it through the concrete wgpu/Bevy capture paths for non-MToon/PBR fallback materials. During the three-vrm comparison, the generated MToon light fixture confirmed that three-vrm's WebGL `MToonMaterial` does not accept `aoMap`/`aoMapIntensity` ShaderMaterial properties, so Rust deliberately keeps MToon occlusion disabled for parity while still preserving and applying occlusion on glTF fallback materials. The generated light fixture now includes an `ambient-ao-ignored` swatch to guard that behavior; `just render-parity-mtoon-light-generated` remains green with selected `rgb-interior1px` PSNR wgpu `54.4445` and Bevy `51.0870`.
- Delegated the routine coverage snapshot refresh to `gpt-5.3-codex-spark`, reviewed the docs diff, and kept the current fixture-coverage narrative intact. The refreshed `cargo llvm-cov` snapshot records workspace line coverage at `82.22%`, `vrm-io` line coverage at `77.87%`, and `vrm-adapter-bevy` line coverage at `94.47%`.
- Added a `rgb-all` render PSNR metric for transparent-background parity sweeps. It compares RGB over the full canvas while the existing alpha-mismatch gate checks transparency separately, avoiding the low `rgb-visible` floor caused by one-pixel silhouette set differences on real transparent captures. `just render-parity-real-transparent` now uses transparent background, `rgb-all`, fail-under `27`, and alpha-mismatch tolerance `64`; the current six-fixture sweep passes with selected PSNR Seed-san wgpu `27.4709`/Bevy `27.3634`, constraint wgpu `33.6106`/Bevy `33.6050`, UV animation wgpu `34.8985`/Bevy `34.8819`, expression masks around `39.29-39.30`, and Alicia VRM0 wgpu `27.7958`/Bevy `27.7923`. Alpha mismatches remain small: Seed-san `25/32`, constraint `11/11`, UV animation `0/0`, expression masks `12/12`, and Alicia `32/32`.
- Re-unified the default render-parity visual-review background on `opaque-black` after the reported three-vrm/wgpu/Bevy transparency mismatch. The three-vrm browser capture, local Rust CI script, wgpu capture example, Bevy capture example, and Justfile render-parity recipes now default to the same opaque background, while `--render-background transparent` remains available for explicit alpha-mask audits. The six-fixture `just render-parity-samples` sweep now reports `transparent=0 opaque=65536 partial=0` and alpha mismatches `0` for three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy on every fixture; selected `rgb-visible` PSNR is Seed-san wgpu `27.4714`, Seed-san Bevy `27.3668`, constraint sample wgpu/Bevy `33.6101`, UV animation sample wgpu/Bevy `34.9081`, mask samples wgpu/Bevy `39.3059` and `39.3063`, and Alicia VRM0 wgpu/Bevy `27.7987`.
- Tightened the concrete wgpu and Bevy MToon color path against three-vrm: `shadeMultiplyTexture` no longer falls back to the main/base texture when absent, and matcap texture transforms are now applied to the generated sphere UVs before sampling. The current official sample sweep is numerically unchanged because those fixtures either provide the relevant textures or use identity transforms, but the renderer paths now match three-vrm's shader policy for broader MToon material fixtures.
- Replaced the hard-coded MToon rim-light mix constant in the wgpu and Bevy capture shaders with the configured indirect light term (`1.0 + pbrAmbient`). This keeps the current numeric default equivalent to the three-vrm capture's `DirectionalLight(Math.PI)` plus `AmbientLight(0.1)` non-physical rim accumulator while making the remaining exact light accumulation work parameter-driven.
- Added an explicit `--mtoon-light-accumulation {tuned,three-vrm}` switch to the wgpu capture, Bevy capture, and local Rust CI runner. The default `tuned` mode preserves the current PSNR-oriented ambient proxy, while `three-vrm` maps the uniform pack to three-vrm's WebGL MToon accumulator shape: direct diffuse stays light-color/PI normalized, indirect diffuse uses `pbrAmbient`, and rim lighting uses the same direct-plus-indirect accumulator exposed by the previous rim-light parameterization. A Seed-san opaque-black smoke run in `three-vrm` mode passes alpha parity but drops selected `rgb-visible` PSNR to wgpu `25.1377` and Bevy `25.0714`, confirming the source-closer accumulator is now testable but the tuned mode remains the better default until remaining geometry/color-space blockers are closed.
- Added a source-like generated transparent MToon fixture path for broader alpha/blend parity without committing binary assets. `tools/render-parity/generate-transparent-fixture.rs` writes `.external-fixtures/generated/transparent-blend.vrm.gltf`, and `just render-parity-transparent-generated` renders it against three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy on a transparent background with zero alpha-mismatch tolerance. The first smoke run reports identical alpha buckets across all three renderers (`transparent=512 opaque=0 partial=65024`, mismatches `0`); it also exposed and fixed a wgpu capture policy bug where glTF `BLEND` materials without MToon `transparentWithZWrite` kept depth writes enabled and failed to accumulate overlapping alpha.
- Added the external UniVRM Alicia VRM0 fixture to the render-parity sample sweep to keep broader legacy transparent material behavior under PSNR/alpha-mask review without committing the binary. A transparent-background smoke run reports three-vrm alpha counts `53752/11784/0`, wgpu/Bevy `53768/11768/0`, alpha mismatches `32`, full-RGBA PSNR `28.6419`, and selected `rgb-visible` PSNR `20.3498` for both Rust renderers.
- Fixed alpha-test output parity in the concrete wgpu and Bevy MToon capture shaders. Masked materials now output fully opaque alpha after the cutoff discard, matching three-vrm/three.js material behavior instead of preserving the sampled texture alpha. A smoke sweep over the official `VRMC_vrm_expressions_isBinary_{Overridden,Overrides}` samples now passes the transparent alpha-mask validation with zero partial-alpha pixels and only 12 alpha-mask edge mismatches per renderer/sample. `just render-parity-samples` now includes those two mask-heavy official fixtures.
- Completed the VRM0 transparent render-queue remap slice. `vrm-sans-io` now builds the same transparent and transparent-Z-write queue maps as three-vrm's v0 compat plugin, compacting VRM0 transparent queues into the VRM1 offset ranges (`-9..0` and `0..9`) instead of preserving raw legacy render queue deltas. A generated compatibility test now covers multiple transparent queues, transparent Z-write queues, and the three-vrm behavior that a legacy renderQueue value alone does not make a material transparent without the alpha keyword/shader classification.
- Verified the transparent render-parity contract after the three-vrm/wgpu/Bevy mismatch report. `just render-parity-samples` regenerated the official sample sweep with `--render-background transparent`; the three-vrm reference now reports transparent/opaque/partial counts of Seed-san `52648/12888/0`, constraint sample `55050/10486/0`, and UV animation sample `53760/11776/0`, matching the same alpha policy as the wgpu and Bevy artifacts.
- Moved VRM0 MToon compatibility mapping closer to three-vrm's `VRMMaterialsV0CompatPlugin`: legacy material colors now apply gamma EOTF, `_ShadeShift`/`_ShadeToony` use the v0-to-v1 conversion, `_IndirectLightIntensity` maps to GI equalization, outline width is converted from centimeters to meters, outline lighting mix honors `_OutlineColorMode`, alpha keywords influence the alpha queue, transparent Z-write requires an actual transparent material, and UV animation Y scroll is flipped. The generated and external fixture tests now assert those normalized values.
- Added explicit PSNR metric selection to the render-parity comparator and local runner. `tools/render-parity/compare-psnr.mjs` now accepts `--metric rgba|rgb-all|rgb-opaque|rgb-visible|rgb-interior1px`, records the selected metric in reports, and applies `--fail-under` to that metric. `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-parity` defaults to `--render-psnr-metric rgb-visible`, matching the transparent background contract so fully transparent pixels do not dominate compatibility thresholds. A smoke run with `--render-fail-under 25` verified that the local runner passes `--metric rgb-visible` to both wgpu and Bevy comparisons.
- Switched the default render-parity background contract back to transparent across the local runner, the standalone three-vrm browser capture, and both concrete wgpu/Bevy capture examples. This keeps the three-vrm reference, wgpu output, and Bevy output aligned when reviewing PNGs over checkerboard backgrounds. `opaque-black` remains available as an explicit `--render-background opaque-black`/just argument for experiments that need fully opaque alpha. The transparent official-sample sweep reports Seed-san alpha counts of three-vrm `52648/12888/0`, wgpu `52643/12893/0`, and Bevy `52644/12892/0` transparent/opaque/partial pixels. Current transparent full-RGBA PSNR is Seed-san wgpu `28.4228`, Seed-san Bevy `28.2468`, constraint sample wgpu/Bevy `34.3346`, UV animation time `0` wgpu/Bevy `36.1575`, and UV animation time `1.0` wgpu/Bevy `35.9209`; the selected `rgb-visible` PSNR is Seed-san wgpu `20.4135`, Seed-san Bevy `20.3099`, constraint sample wgpu/Bevy `25.6527`, UV animation time `0` wgpu/Bevy `27.4533`, and UV animation time `1.0` wgpu/Bevy `27.2167`.
- Added screen-coordinate MToon outline scaling to both concrete render capture paths. The wgpu and Bevy CPU outline-mesh builders now apply the same distance/projection factor that three-vrm applies for `outlineWidthMode = screenCoordinates` (`vViewPosition.z / projectionMatrix[1].y`) while preserving the existing world-coordinate path. The current official render sweep is unchanged because the local fixtures use world-coordinate outlines or no outlines, but future screen-coordinate fixtures can now be compared without falling back to world-width expansion.
- Added time-aware MToon UV animation coverage to the render parity harness. `tools/render-parity/three-vrm-browser-capture.mjs`, `tools/ci/local-ci.rs`, `examples/wgpu_render_capture.rs`, and `examples/bevy_render_capture.rs` now accept `mtoon-time`/`render-mtoon-time`; the wgpu and Bevy capture shaders apply three-vrm-style UV scroll/rotation before slot-specific texture transforms and bind the UV animation mask texture path. `just render-parity-samples` now includes the official `VRMC_materials_mtoon_UV_Animation_Test.vrm` at time `0`, and `just render-parity-uv-animation` writes a separate time `1.0` sweep under `.external-fixtures/render-parity-uv-animation/`. Current PSNR is UV animation time `0` wgpu/Bevy `36.1575` and time `1.0` wgpu/Bevy `35.9209`, with alpha mismatches `0`.
- Wired retained MToon/glTF texture transforms into the concrete wgpu and Bevy render-capture paths. The wgpu example now sends slot-specific UV transforms through a per-primitive material uniform buffer, while the Bevy custom material mirrors the same base/shade/shading-shift/normal/rim/emissive transform uniforms in WGSL; outline-width texture sampling also applies the retained transform on the CPU side. The official two-fixture render sweep remains stable because those fixtures only contain identity transforms: Seed-san wgpu `28.7208`, Seed-san Bevy `28.6162`, and constraint sample wgpu/Bevy `34.8595`, with alpha mismatches `0`.
- Added static texture-transform retention for MToon/glTF material parity: `VRMC_materials_mtoon` texture infos now preserve nested `KHR_texture_transform`/extras in `vrm-protocol`, `vrm-sans-io` maps those transforms into `MtoonTextureTransformSet`, and `vrm-io` enables `gltf`'s `KHR_texture_transform` feature so glTF base/normal/emissive texture transforms are extracted and inherited by resolved MToon materials. The current official two-fixture render sweep is unchanged because the sampled transforms are identity in those files, but future renderer work can now apply the same slot-specific UV transforms that three-vrm exposes to its shader uniforms.
- Previously unified the render-parity background policy around `opaque-black`: `tools/render-parity/three-vrm-browser-capture.mjs`, `examples/wgpu_render_capture.rs`, and `examples/bevy_render_capture.rs` accepted the same `--background opaque-black|transparent` switch, and `tools/ci/local-ci.rs` passed `--render-background opaque-black` by default. That `just render-parity-samples` run reported `transparent/opaque/partial = 0/65536/0` and alpha mismatches `0` for three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy on both Seed-san and the constraint sample. PSNR was Seed-san wgpu `28.7208`, Seed-san Bevy `28.6162`, and constraint sample wgpu/Bevy `34.8595`.
- Tightened render parity alpha handling earlier in the parity loop by adding alpha-mask validation for transparent captures; that path stayed available through `--render-background transparent` while the then-current review path used `opaque-black`.
- Documented the canonical render review image locations under `.external-fixtures/render-parity/{three-vrm,wgpu,bevy}/`.
- Unified the three-vrm direct PNG capture path with the RGBA readback path: `tools/render-parity/three-vrm-browser-capture.mjs --png-out` now encodes PNG bytes directly from `gl.readPixels` RGBA data instead of round-tripping through a browser canvas data URL. A 128px Seed-san transparent-mode smoke capture produced transparent/opaque/partial counts of `15057/1327/0`.
- Routed the wgpu and Bevy render capture mesh baking through a shared `HeadlessSceneState` plus `VrmRuntimeDriver` tick so static render artifacts include the same renderer-agnostic runtime pose, constraint ordering, spring rest capture, MToon pass hints, emissive strength, and VRM0 root-orientation path used by engine adapters. The current official-sample PSNR is effectively unchanged (`Seed-san` wgpu `28.2117`, Bevy `27.9992`; constraint sample wgpu `34.2094`, Bevy `33.8841`), which narrows the next parity work toward exact MToon lighting/color/outline behavior rather than missing static runtime application.
- Moved wgpu and Bevy outline rendering closer to three-vrm by running outline geometry through the same MToon shader path and applying `outlineColorFactor * mix(1, litColor, outlineLightingMixFactor)` at the end of the fragment pass instead of using a flat unlit outline material. The official-sample sweep improved to `Seed-san` wgpu `28.3645`, Bevy `28.1909`; constraint sample wgpu/Bevy `34.2969`.
- Matched three.js double-sided normal behavior in both render capture shaders by carrying a double-sided material flag into wgpu and Bevy MToon fragments and flipping normal/TBN on back-facing fragments. The current two-fixture sweep is visually and numerically unchanged (`Seed-san` wgpu `28.3645`, Bevy `28.1909`; constraint sample wgpu/Bevy `34.2969`), so the remaining delta is still dominated by MToon light/color and outline-space details rather than alpha or double-sided state.
- Added renderer-facing glTF metallic/roughness extraction plus a small GGX-based non-MToon fallback path for the wgpu and Bevy capture shaders, and wired glTF emissive textures into both renderers. This moves Seed-san's non-MToon metal/emissive materials closer to three.js `MeshStandardMaterial`; the local sweep improved Seed-san to wgpu `28.4228` and Bevy `28.2468`, while the all-MToon constraint sample remains wgpu/Bevy `34.2969`.
- Recreated the render-parity output directories at the start of each local run and added PNG-vs-RGBA byte validation so stale direct-capture images cannot make three-vrm look opaque while wgpu/Bevy look transparent. The current canonical PNG alpha counts are `Seed-san` three-vrm `52648/12888/0`, wgpu `52643/12893/0`, Bevy `52644/12892/0`, and constraint sample three-vrm `55050/10486/0`, wgpu/Bevy `55055/10481/0` (`transparent/opaque/partial`). PSNR reports now also include alpha counts plus RGB-only opaque/visible/interior metrics.
- Matched another three-vrm material-loading detail: VRM1 MToon materials now inherit glTF `baseColorFactor`, `baseColorTexture`, `normalTexture`, and `emissiveFactor` in `vrm-io` before the model is resolved, mirroring GLTFLoader material params plus the MToon extension overlay. The wgpu and Bevy capture examples use the same merged data. This fixes missing MToon emissive contribution in samples such as `VRM1_Constraint_Twist_Sample.vrm`, raising its local wgpu/Bevy PSNR from `34.2969` to `34.3346` while keeping Seed-san at wgpu `28.4228` and Bevy `28.2468`.

## 2026-04-29

- Created workspace skeleton.
- Added `vrm-protocol` schema coverage for VRM0, VRMC_vrm, VRMC_springBone, VRMC_node_constraint, VRMC_materials_mtoon, and VRMC_vrm_animation.
- Added `vrm-core` domain types, state markers, newtype references, feature ADT, humanoid/expression/lookAt/spring/constraint/material/animation models.
- Added `vrm-sans-io` conversion and validation.
- Added `vrm-io` glTF import and extension extraction.
- Added `vrm-runtime` expression, constraint ordering, spring ordering, lookAt math, and track sampling.
- Added `vrm-adapter` engine integration traits and mock adapter test.
- Added generated in-test glTF/VRM sample data that is not stored as repository model assets.
- Added IO tests that load generated VRMC_vrm, VRMC_springBone, VRMC_node_constraint, and VRMC_materials_mtoon data.
- Expanded validation to expression node/material references and spring collider/collider-group references.
- Switched runtime expression keys to VRM canonical names such as `blink` and `lookLeft`.
- Added `load_vrm_from_path` and ignored external fixture directory tests using `VRM_RS_FIXTURE_DIR`.
- Ignored `.external-fixtures/` for local official sample downloads.
- Downloaded official/local-testing samples into `.external-fixtures/official/` via Spark and verified `cargo test -p vrm-io loads_external_fixture_directory -- --ignored`.
- Added VRMA glTF animation channel extraction for humanoid rotation, hips translation, expression scalar tracks, and lookAt rotation tracks.
- Added `VrmDocument::animations` to preserve multiple glTF animation clips while keeping `animation` as the first/legacy convenience value.
- Added VRM0 `firstPerson`/lookAt compatibility mapping and `secondaryAnimation` to spring bone normalization.
- Added pure spring particle Verlet-style stepping and sphere/capsule/plane collision correction helpers in `vrm-runtime`.
- Added renderer-agnostic node constraint solvers for rotation, roll, and aim constraints.
- Added MToon render queue hints, `render_order()`, and `outline_enabled()` helpers.
- Added VRMA rest-pose aware rotation remapping, rest hips position capture, and hips translation mapping through the hips parent rest matrix.
- Added `sample_vrm_animation` frame application helpers in `vrm-adapter` so engine adapters can apply sampled humanoid rotations, hips translation, and expression binds through traits.
- Added renderer-agnostic LookAt expression weight calculation for `lookLeft`, `lookRight`, `lookUp`, and `lookDown`.
- Added first-person visibility adapter helpers that map VRM mesh annotations to engine-provided node visibility.
- Added spring bone collider group resolution, world/center-space collider conversion, and adapter helpers for collecting simulation-space colliders from engine world transforms.
- Added node constraint adapter application helpers that read current/rest transforms through traits and write solved local rotations back to the target engine.
- Added spring tail-to-local-rotation solving and adapter writeback helper for applying solved spring tails to engine joint transforms.
- Added `ConstraintRestMap` capture helper so adapters can snapshot node constraint rest rotations from initial local transforms.
- Added high-level spring bone adapter stepping that gathers colliders, advances particles, and writes joint rotations through engine transform traits.
- Expanded VRM0 compatibility mapping for blend shape material value binds and legacy thumb intermediate bone aliases.
- Expanded VRM0 blend shape material value mapping so texture transform properties such as `_MainTex_ST` become `TextureTransform` binds instead of generic material color binds.
- Added renderer-agnostic MToon texture slots in core and mapped VRM0 texture properties plus VRMC_materials_mtoon texture infos into them.
- Finished the first explicit VRM0 orientation compensation layer as `Vrm0Compatibility::orientation_correction`, with adapter helper support for applying it to an engine root node.
- Added renderer-agnostic MToon pipeline hints for alpha mode, culling, depth write/test, blend, render order, and outline pass hints.
- Added `VrmRuntimeDriver` in `vrm-adapter` to combine VRM0 orientation, animation frame application, runtime expression events, constraints, spring stepping, MToon pipeline hints, and first-person visibility in one tick.
- Added MToon pipeline pass helper and adapter trait for renderer-side pipeline selection without shader generation.
- Added the first automation pass for fmt/test/clippy and non-threshold `cargo-llvm-cov` summary coverage; later work removed GitHub-hosted CI and moved the gate into the local Rust runner.
- Expanded external fixture tests from load-only checks to semantic assertions for VRM metadata/humanoid/material/spring/constraints and VRMA tracks.
- Expanded protocol roundtrip tests for VRM0, spring bone, node constraint, MToon, VRMA, unknown extensions, and invalid extension errors.
- Hardened `VrmRuntimeDriver` so VRM0 root orientation compensation is applied once per driver instead of compounding every tick.
- Changed VRMA/sample humanoid hips translation adapter writeback to set absolute local translation for the sampled frame rather than accumulating deltas.
- Added per-extension `specVersion` validation for extracted `VRMC_springBone`, `VRMC_node_constraint`, and `VRMC_materials_mtoon`, accepting three-vrm-compatible `1.0` and `1.0-beta`.
- Added archived `VRMC_materials_hdr_emissiveMultiplier` protocol/IO/sans-IO/core/adapter support, mapping it to renderer-facing emissive intensity.
- Fixed VRM1 material extension mapping to preserve glTF material indices in `VrmDocument::materials`.
- Raised generated/unit/doc coverage to 81.02% line coverage and added a conservative CI `cargo-llvm-cov --fail-under-lines 70` gate.
- Added type-state style humanoid pose types for raw/normalized and absolute/rest-relative poses.
- Added `HumanoidPoseRig` in `vrm-adapter` for raw pose get/set/reset, normalized pose get/set/reset, and normalized-to-raw writeback through engine transform traits.

- Added `KHR_materials_emissive_strength` protocol/IO/sans-IO/core/adapter support and made it take precedence over archived `VRMC_materials_hdr_emissiveMultiplier`.

- Raised generated/unit/doc coverage to 83.70% line coverage after parity API and Bevy skeleton tests.
- Added `CenterSpringRuntimeState`, `SpringRestMap`, `step_spring_bone_system_parity`, and `VrmRuntimeDriver::tick_with_spring_parity` so engine adapters can drive spring bones through the center-space three-vrm parity path instead of the older world-space particle path.
- Spring rest capture now records sparse spring-chain children, first scene-child fallback, center-space initial tails, center node references, and the VRM0 7cm final-joint fallback separately from mutable runtime state.

- Reframed the real-sample model-body parity sweep around
  `rgb-nonblack-interior1px`, because the previous `rgb-nonblack` guard was
  dominated by one-pixel silhouette/raster fill-rule deltas on Seed-san Bevy.
  `just render-parity-samples-nonblack` now writes
  `.external-fixtures/render-parity-samples-nonblack-interior/` and enforces
  `>= 27.4 dB` on the interior model-body metric. The six-fixture sweep passes:
  Seed-san wgpu `30.0461 dB` / Bevy `27.5065 dB`, constraint sample wgpu
  `29.7271 dB` / Bevy `29.7174 dB`, UV animation wgpu `28.6651 dB` / Bevy
  `28.6173 dB`, expression masks around `50-51 dB`, and Alicia VRM0 wgpu
  `34.4442 dB` / Bevy `34.3709 dB`, all with zero alpha mismatches.
- Re-measured coverage after moving tangentless normal-map tangent generation into
  `vrm-io::generate_tangents` and refreshing workspace coverage on 2026-05-31:
  workspace line coverage is 84.41%, and `vrm-adapter-bevy` line coverage is
  94.42%.
- Attempted to delegate the latest coverage docs refresh to `gpt-5.3-codex-spark`, but the requested model was at capacity. The main agent ran the same documented JSON summary/update flow locally and restored the surrounding testing documentation after reviewing the generated diff.
- Attempted to delegate the 2026-05-30 coverage docs refresh to `gpt-5.3-codex-spark`, but the requested model was at capacity. The main agent ran the same documented JSON summary/update flow locally and reviewed the resulting docs diff.
- Created and pushed the public GitHub repository at `https://github.com/Sanzentyo/vrm-rs`.
- Started P3 render parity work. The new target is optional external-fixture local automation, additional official VRMA parity discovery, non-Bevy adapter implementation depth, concrete wgpu/ash material pipeline examples, and a three-vrm-vs-Rust render parity harness with PSNR plus visual-review artifacts.
- Added optional external-fixture automation. It downloads documented fixtures into ignored workspace paths, builds a pinned three-vrm checkout, regenerates spring/constraint/VRMA golden JSON, and runs exact ignored parity tests without committing binaries.
- Added `docs/vrma-fixture-discovery.md` after checking `pixiv/three-vrm`, `vrm-c/vrm-specification`, `vrm-c/UniVRM`, and `vrm-c/bvh2vrma` trees. Stable upstream VRMA coverage is still `test.vrma`; Spark also found branch-only experimental `idle_loop.vrma`, which is useful external-only parity data but should not be vendored without explicit review.
- Added `HeadlessSceneState` to `vrm-adapter` as a non-Bevy, renderer-neutral adapter implementation. It covers graph/transform/world-matrix access, constraint rest capture, morph/material/MToon/emissive writes, first-person visibility, and lookAt storage, with a runtime-driver test demonstrating framework-free integration for wgpu/ash/custom engines.
- Deepened `examples/mtoon_renderer_skeletons.rs` from simple pipeline keys into concrete wgpu-like and ash-like descriptors covering pass order, alpha/depth/cull state, render order, texture/sampler bindings, uniform layout sizing, shader entry naming, and material texture binding tables.
- Expanded VRMA external parity with Spark's branch-only `idle_loop.vrma` finding from pixiv/three-vrm. The clip remains external-only and exposed a missing three-vrm behavior: hips translation tracks are scaled by target normalized hips height divided by the VRMA source rest hips height before normalized-to-raw writeback. `VrmAnimationFrame` now carries the source rest hips position from `sample_vrm_animation`, and `apply_vrma_humanoid_frame` applies that scale for parity.
- Started the render parity harness with `tools/render-parity/compare-psnr.mjs` and `docs/render-parity.md`. The first slice defines a renderer-neutral RGBA JSON artifact, PSNR/MSE/max-delta report generation, initial thresholds, and `.external-fixtures/render-parity/` artifact policy before adding concrete three-vrm/Bevy/wgpu capture paths.
- Historical note: the removed manual GitHub Actions external fixture job was once verified on `master` in run `26633741797`. The repository now uses the local Rust script instead of GitHub-hosted CI.
- Added `tools/render-parity/three-vrm-browser-capture.mjs`, an optional Playwright/Chromium reference capture path that serves a local three-vrm build and VRM fixture, renders a fixed camera/light frame in WebGL, and writes RGBA JSON for PSNR comparison.
- Extended the three-vrm browser capture to optionally write a PNG visual-review artifact and verified a local Seed-san reference capture plus self-PSNR report. Added renderer-facing mesh primitive extraction to `vrm-io` (`LoadedVrm::meshes` and `GltfNodeRest::mesh`) so Bevy/wgpu capture paths can build real mesh buffers from the loaded VRM instead of placeholder geometry.
- Added `examples/wgpu_render_capture.rs`, an offscreen wgpu capture path that renders real `LoadedVrm::meshes` primitive buffers and writes RGBA JSON plus PNG artifacts under `.external-fixtures/render-parity/`. The local Seed-san smoke run produced a first wgpu-vs-three-vrm PSNR report at `8.78 dB`, documenting the remaining texture/skinning/MToon shading gap rather than claiming visual parity.
- Extended `vrm-io` with decoded image metadata, texture-to-image mapping, and glTF PBR material base-color texture data. The wgpu capture now samples base-color textures and uses the three-vrm-facing camera convention; the Seed-san local PSNR baseline rose to `9.64 dB`.
- Added glTF skin extraction (`LoadedVrm::skins`, node skin refs, `JOINTS_0`, `WEIGHTS_0`, inverse bind matrices) and CPU rest-skinning in the wgpu capture path. The local Seed-san PSNR baseline is now `9.70 dB`, with the remaining gap concentrated in MToon lighting/material ordering/expression state rather than basic textured mesh input.
- Wired MToon-derived render order, cull mode, alpha mask discard, blend state, and depth-write policy into the wgpu render capture pipelines. The Seed-san baseline remains `9.70 dB`, but alpha/cull/material-order parity is no longer hard-coded to a single back-face opaque pipeline.
- Fixed the three-vrm browser capture RGBA row convention by flipping WebGL `readPixels` output into top-left order, and fixed the canvas CSS size used for PNG review. The corrected local Seed-san wgpu-vs-three-vrm PSNR baseline rose to `17.73 dB`.
- Added a first MToon-like shading approximation to the wgpu render capture: shade color, shading shift/toony, GI-biased ambient, directional light direction matching the three-vrm capture, and effective KHR/HDR emissive strength now feed the shader. The local Seed-san baseline rose to `20.75 dB`; remaining parity work is exact MToon light accumulation, secondary MToon textures, outlines, normal maps, and runtime expression state.
- Added `examples/bevy_render_capture.rs`, a Bevy 0.18.1 headless offscreen capture path using `RenderTarget::Image`, a render-graph readback node, real `LoadedVrm::meshes`, decoded images, and an unlit `StandardMaterial` baseline. The initial local Seed-san Bevy-vs-three-vrm PSNR baseline was `10.60 dB`; this proves Bevy readback integration but still lacks MToon shading, outlines, camera/background parity, and runtime expression state.
- Removed the GitHub Actions workflow at user request and added `tools/ci/local-ci.rs`, a Rust `cargo +nightly -Zscript` runner for the old fmt/test/clippy/coverage gate plus optional local external-fixture parity (`--external-fixtures`).
- Started improving the Bevy capture parity baseline by baking glTF rest transforms and CPU rest-skinning into generated Bevy meshes, then applying MToon pipeline hints for render order, alpha mode, cull mode, and double-sided policy through `StandardMaterial`. The local Seed-san Bevy-vs-three-vrm baseline is now `10.67 dB`; the remaining large visual delta is camera/background framing plus missing MToon shading and runtime state.
- Matched the Bevy capture projection to the three-vrm/wgpu reference camera by explicitly setting a 30 degree perspective FOV, 1:1 aspect, and `0.1..20.0` clip range. The Seed-san Bevy alpha bounding box now matches the three-vrm reference (`252x221`), and local Bevy-vs-three-vrm PSNR rose to `23.64 dB`; the remaining gap is now dominated by stock `StandardMaterial` shading versus MToon/runtime state rather than framing.
- Extended `tools/ci/local-ci.rs` with `--render-parity`, which regenerates the Seed-san three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy RGBA/PNG artifacts plus PSNR reports under `.external-fixtures/render-parity/`. The local reused-fixture run produced the current wgpu `20.75 dB` and Bevy `23.64 dB` reports.
- Added local render visual-review output: `--render-parity` now writes `.external-fixtures/render-parity/visual-review.html`, showing the three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy PNGs side-by-side with the PSNR JSON reports for human review without committing generated artifacts.
- Added render parity diff heatmaps to the local Rust runner. `--render-parity` now writes amplified RGB/alpha delta PNGs under `.external-fixtures/render-parity/diff/` and includes them in `visual-review.html`; the reused Seed-san run still reports wgpu `20.75 dB` and Bevy `23.64 dB`.
- Improved renderer parity baselines from the heatmap pass. The wgpu capture now matches the three-vrm camera clip range and directional-light vector convention, raising Seed-san from `20.75 dB` to `24.32 dB`. The Bevy capture keeps stock `StandardMaterial` but bakes an approximate MToon light response into vertex colors, raising Seed-san from `23.64 dB` to `23.77 dB`.
- Added first-pass world-coordinate MToon outline expansion to both capture paths. The wgpu Seed-san baseline rose to `25.89 dB`, and the Bevy baseline rose to `24.07 dB`; the remaining outline gap is exact width texture handling and shader-level details.
- Added outline width multiply texture sampling for expanded outline capture geometry. The wgpu Seed-san baseline rose again to `25.98 dB`; the Bevy baseline is effectively unchanged at `24.07 dB` but now follows the same outline width input path.
- Tuned the capture-only MToon lighting approximation after measuring that both Rust render paths were roughly 9% brighter than the three-vrm reference on opaque shared pixels. The wgpu Seed-san baseline rose to `27.50 dB`, and the Bevy baseline rose to `24.98 dB`.
- Added secondary MToon texture bindings to the wgpu capture for `shadeMultiplyTexture` and `matcapTexture`. On Seed-san, most shade textures alias the main texture, but matcap improves arm material matching slightly; the local wgpu-vs-three-vrm baseline is now `27.52 dB` while Bevy remains `24.98 dB`.
- Added VRM1 `shadingShiftTexture` plus scale to the core/sans-IO MToon model and propagated it through adapter material descriptors, Bevy texture plans, and wgpu capture bindings. The wgpu capture also now computes matcap UVs from the camera view direction and applies parametric rim lighting/rim multiply inputs. The local Seed-san wgpu-vs-three-vrm baseline is now `27.63 dB`; Bevy remains `24.98 dB`.
- Extended renderer-facing glTF material extraction with alpha mode, alpha cutoff, and double-sided flags, then wired those inputs into the wgpu and Bevy capture material policies. Seed-san's current static frame remains wgpu `27.63 dB` and Bevy `24.98 dB`, but transparent/double-sided material handling is no longer implicit in the capture paths.
- Confirmed that no `.github` workflow files are tracked, then hardened `tools/ci/local-ci.rs` so the local CI-equivalent runner fails fast if GitHub Actions workflow YAML files reappear. Updated agent/testing/TODO docs to make the Rust script the canonical gate.
- Extended renderer-facing glTF material extraction with emissive factor, emissive texture index, and `KHR_materials_emissive_strength`. The wgpu render capture now uses a Lambert-style fallback for non-MToon glTF materials and nudges Seed-san from `27.63 dB` to `27.64 dB`; Bevy consumes the same emissive values in its baked color path but remains `24.98 dB` until a custom MToon/runtime render path replaces stock `StandardMaterial`.
- Fed MToon `shadingShiftTexture` into the Bevy capture's baked vertex-color toon threshold while preserving Bevy's main texture path. The local Seed-san Bevy-vs-three-vrm baseline nudged from `24.98 dB` to `25.00 dB`; wgpu remains `27.64 dB`.
- Normalized capture outline pass ordering to match `MtoonPipelineHints`: both wgpu and Bevy outline primitives now render at base material order plus one. Re-running the Seed-san local render parity loop leaves wgpu at `27.64 dB` and Bevy at `25.00 dB`, so this closes a pass-order mismatch without changing the current fixture's measured PSNR.
- Exposed glTF material normal textures and primitive tangents through `vrm-io`, then added tangent-space normal map sampling to the wgpu render capture with linear normal texture uploads and capture-local tangent generation for meshes that omit `TANGENT`. The local Seed-san wgpu-vs-three-vrm baseline rose from `27.64 dB` to `27.77 dB`; Bevy remains `25.00 dB` because the current Bevy path still bakes approximate MToon lighting into unlit vertex colors.
- Rejected several Bevy stock-material capture tweaks after measurement: baking normal maps into vertex colors lowered Seed-san Bevy PSNR to `24.99 dB`, flipping the normal-map Y channel lowered it to `24.98 dB`, shrinking Bevy outline width to 75% lowered it to `24.99 dB`, and baking matcap/rim terms lowered it to `24.96 dB`. A wgpu exposure retune from `0.80` to `0.803` also lowered wgpu PSNR to `27.76 dB`. These results point the next Bevy parity work toward a real custom MToon shader/runtime path instead of more vertex-color baking on `StandardMaterial`.
- Added the first Bevy custom MToon material path for `examples/bevy_render_capture.rs` on Bevy 0.18.1. The example now uses `MaterialPlugin`, a source-controlled WGSL shader, and MToon uniform/texture bindings for base, shade, shading shift, matcap, rim, and normal textures, with linear normal texture uploads where glTF tangents are present. The local Seed-san Bevy-vs-three-vrm baseline rose from `25.00 dB` to `25.06 dB`; the small gain confirms the shader path works but also shows that exact MToon light accumulation, runtime expression state, and outline model details remain the larger blockers.
- Matched the capture shaders' rim/matcap composition more closely to three-vrm by adding matcap into the rim term before `rimMultiplyTexture` and `rimLightingMix`, raising the local Seed-san wgpu baseline from `27.77 dB` to `27.98 dB` and Bevy from `25.06 dB` to `25.08 dB`. Bevy-side generated tangents for normal-mapped primitives that omit glTF `TANGENT`, plus removal of the measured-worse material depth-bias experiment, raise the current Bevy baseline to `25.20 dB`.
- Replaced hand-written Rust CLI argument parsing with `clap` for the local Rust CI runner and the wgpu/Bevy render capture examples. The local CI runner still accepts the documented `cargo +nightly -Zscript tools/ci/local-ci.rs -- --render-parity` form by filtering the cargo-script separator before invoking clap.
- Added a root `Justfile` with `just ci`, `just ci-external`, `just render-parity`, `just render-parity-full`, and `just coverage` wrappers. These keep the Rust script as the implementation source of truth while making the common local gate and render-parity commands easier to run.
- Extended local render parity beyond the Seed-san-only path. `tools/ci/local-ci.rs --render-parity` now accepts repeated `--render-fixture` flags, writes per-fixture RGBA/PNG/report/diff artifacts, and builds a multi-section `visual-review.html`. The first two-fixture run covers `Seed-san.vrm` and `VRM1_Constraint_Twist_Sample.vrm`; the constraint sample reports wgpu `33.87 dB` and Bevy `26.89 dB`.
- Unified render-parity PNG alpha handling. The three-vrm reference PNG is now encoded from the same transparent RGBA readback buffer used for PSNR, instead of using a Playwright element screenshot that composited the canvas background differently from the wgpu/Bevy PNG artifacts.
- Added CPU-generated mip chains and glTF-like repeat/linear/mipmap-nearest sampler policy to the wgpu and Bevy render capture texture uploads. The local two-fixture render sweep now reports Seed-san at wgpu `28.17 dB` and Bevy `25.26 dB`, and `VRM1_Constraint_Twist_Sample.vrm` at wgpu `33.93 dB` and Bevy `26.90 dB`.
- Made render-capture MToon lighting coefficients configurable through clap in `tools/ci/local-ci.rs`, `examples/wgpu_render_capture.rs`, and `examples/bevy_render_capture.rs`. The local CI runner no longer passes those approximation knobs into the three-vrm reference path.
- Tightened transparent PNG parity by making the three-vrm browser capture request straight alpha (`premultipliedAlpha: false`) and by having the local runner encode all three renderer PNGs from their RGBA JSON artifacts with the same Rust PNG encoder. The two-fixture PNG alpha check now shows transparent background for three-vrm, wgpu, and Bevy artifacts.
- Retuned the default capture-only MToon lighting approximation to exposure `0.78`, ambient base `0.12`, ambient GI scale `0.20`, and PBR ambient `0.03183099`. The local two-fixture sweep now reports Seed-san at wgpu `28.21 dB` and Bevy `25.27 dB`, and `VRM1_Constraint_Twist_Sample.vrm` at wgpu `34.21 dB` and Bevy `26.91 dB`.
- Disabled Bevy camera MSAA in the render capture to match the antialias-disabled three-vrm/wgpu reference condition. This removes partial-alpha edge pixels from Bevy artifacts and raises the local two-fixture Bevy sweep to Seed-san `28.00 dB` and `VRM1_Constraint_Twist_Sample.vrm` `33.88 dB`, with the remaining delta now mostly RGB/MToon rather than alpha resolve behavior.
- Matched generated transparent MToon blend color parity. The wgpu and Bevy capture paths now manually encode shader output to sRGB before UNORM target blending, matching three-vrm's `postCorrection()`-before-framebuffer blend behavior on transparent backgrounds. The wgpu path uses `LessEqual` depth compare so equal-depth `transparentWithZWrite` layers match three.js, while the Bevy capture carries `transparentWithZWrite` into the custom material pipeline and compensates its transparent tie-break spawn order. `just render-parity-transparent-generated` now reports wgpu `rgb-visible = Infinity` with max channel delta `0`, Bevy `rgb-visible = 52.9020 dB` with max channel delta `1`, and alpha mismatches `0` for both renderers. The broader six-fixture opaque sweep remains stable at Seed-san wgpu `27.4709 dB`, Seed-san Bevy `27.3634 dB`, constraint sample wgpu `33.6106 dB`, constraint sample Bevy `33.6050 dB`, UV animation wgpu `34.8985 dB`, UV animation Bevy `34.8819 dB`, mask samples around `39.29-39.30 dB`, Alicia VRM0 wgpu `27.7958 dB`, Alicia VRM0 Bevy `27.7923 dB`, and alpha mismatches `0` across the sweep.
- Added renderer-facing glTF `COLOR_0` extraction and documented the MToon vertex-color rule in the render path. `vrm-io` now exposes primitive vertex colors, and the wgpu capture applies them only for non-MToon/PBR fallback materials; MToon keeps ignoring vertex colors to match three-vrm and the VRMC_materials_mtoon spec. The generated transparent fixture now includes a `COLOR_0` gradient specifically to guard that MToon-ignore behavior. `just render-parity-transparent-generated` still reports wgpu `rgb-visible = Infinity`, Bevy `rgb-visible = 52.9020 dB`, and alpha mismatches `0`.
- Implemented the three-vrm `V0_COMPAT_SHADE` clamp in the concrete wgpu and Bevy MToon capture shaders. VRM0-compat MToon materials now pack a flag through `emissive.w` and clamp the direct toon contribution with `min(direct, diffuse)`, matching the v0 compatibility shader branch. The six-fixture render sweep remains numerically stable after this exactness fix: selected `rgb-visible` stays at Seed-san wgpu `27.4709 dB`, Seed-san Bevy `27.3634 dB`, constraint sample wgpu `33.6106 dB`, constraint sample Bevy `33.6050 dB`, UV animation wgpu `34.8985 dB`, UV animation Bevy `34.8819 dB`, mask samples around `39.29-39.30 dB`, Alicia VRM0 wgpu `27.7958 dB`, and Alicia VRM0 Bevy `27.7923 dB`; alpha mismatches remain `0`.
- Broadened the generated transparent fixture with an embedded bufferView PNG base-color texture on one MToon `BLEND` layer, while keeping the second layer on `transparentWithZWrite`. This remains source-generated and does not commit binary assets. `just render-parity-transparent-generated` reports identical alpha buckets and alpha mismatches `0`; selected `rgb-visible` is wgpu `53.0238 dB` with max channel delta `1`, and Bevy `49.7151 dB` with max channel delta `2`. Strong high-contrast textured transparent layer ordering still remains a separate Bevy/render-sort blocker.
- Stabilized Bevy high-contrast transparent MToon ordering by extracting a small `Transparent3d` phase-sort tie-break from the same MToon transparent render-order policy used by the wgpu capture and adapter hints. The generated fixture now supports `--palette high-contrast`, and `just render-parity-transparent-high-contrast` reports exact alpha buckets/mismatches `0`; selected `rgb-visible` is wgpu `53.1994 dB` with max channel delta `1`, and Bevy `51.9341 dB` with max channel delta `2`. The normal generated transparent fixture remains stable at wgpu `53.0238 dB`, Bevy `49.7151 dB`, with alpha mismatches `0`, and the broader six-fixture opaque sweep remains unchanged.
- Tightened the explicit `--mtoon-light-accumulation three-vrm` mode so it no longer inherits the PSNR-tuned exposure `0.78`; the reference-shaped accumulator now uses exposure `1.0`, ambient irradiance `pbrAmbient`, no GI-equalization ambient proxy, and the same direct-plus-indirect rim accumulator documented from three-vrm's WebGL shader. `just render-parity-light-three-vrm` now records Seed-san selected `rgb-visible` at wgpu `26.8701 dB`, Bevy `26.7842 dB`, and the constraint sample at wgpu `33.1714 dB`, Bevy `33.1498 dB`. The `tuned` mode remains the default for the broader PSNR sweep until the remaining non-lighting deltas are removed.
- Folded Spark's pessimistic MToon review into the parity backlog. The review confirmed the WebGL shader formulas in three-vrm: `giEqualizationFactor` is declared but not consumed by current `mtoon.frag`, `lightingInfluence` is not a current WebGL shader input, rim/matcap is added after diffuse as `mix(1, reflectedLight.directSpecular, rimLightingMixFactor) * rim`, and `postCorrection()` handles the final three.js output chunks. Bevy now carries explicit `material_flags` and `pbr_params` uniforms instead of hiding V0/PBR controls in color-vector `.w` lanes. wgpu gained a per-primitive `MaterialExtraUniform` at texture bind-group binding `10`, so it also removes those packed `.w` lane semantics without adding inter-stage varyings. Re-running generated transparent parity and the focused three-vrm light mode shows unchanged numbers.
- Added a source-like generated MToon light/color fixture for exact accumulator work without committing binary assets. `tools/render-parity/generate-mtoon-light-fixture.rs` writes `.external-fixtures/generated/mtoon-light.vrm.gltf` with six opaque MToon quads covering forced base lighting, forced shade lighting, ambient-only behavior, parametric rim, matcap rim, and mixed rim/matcap. `just render-parity-mtoon-light-generated` renders it with `--mtoon-light-accumulation three-vrm` into `.external-fixtures/render-parity-mtoon-light-generated/`; the first run reports selected `rgb-visible` PSNR wgpu `28.6300 dB` and Bevy `28.6100 dB`, with full-RGBA PSNR wgpu `29.8794 dB` and Bevy `29.8594 dB`. Visual review at `.external-fixtures/render-parity-mtoon-light-generated/visual-review.html` shows the expected six material swatches and keeps the remaining work focused on exact reflected-light/post-correction/rim-matcap color accumulation rather than fixture availability.
- Reclassified the generated MToon light fixture as a shader-color audit rather than a silhouette audit. The opaque-black run exposed a single vertical one-pixel rasterization coverage delta (`x=231`, 240 pixels) that dominated `rgb-visible` PSNR and produced max channel delta `255`, while the swatch interiors already matched closely. `just render-parity-mtoon-light-generated` now renders the fixture on a transparent background, allows the known edge-only alpha mismatch (`240`, tolerance `512`), selects `rgb-interior1px`, and fails below `50 dB`. The current focused color result is wgpu `54.4445 dB` with max selected channel delta `1`, and Bevy `51.0870 dB` with max selected channel delta `2`; this gives exact MToon light/color work a stable high-signal regression gate while preserving the separate transparent fixture for strict alpha/blend parity.
- Added a source-like generated screen-coordinate MToon outline fixture. `tools/render-parity/generate-screen-outline-fixture.rs` writes `.external-fixtures/generated/screen-outline.vrm.gltf` with a simple VRM1/MToon prism using `outlineWidthMode = screenCoordinates`. `just render-parity-screen-outline-generated` renders it on a transparent background with `rgb-opaque`, so the score covers pixels where both renderers drew the body/outline while reporting the known one-pixel fill-rule alpha edge separately. The first focused run reports alpha mismatches `188` (tolerance `256`), selected PSNR wgpu `Infinity` with max selected channel delta `0`, and Bevy `53.2689 dB` with max selected channel delta `1`. This moves screen-coordinate outline from an unmeasured implemented branch to a repeatable three-vrm/wgpu/Bevy parity guard.
- Added `just render-parity-real-transparent` for transparent-background coverage over the real external sample set instead of only generated fixtures.
