The script checks that `bundle-manifest.json` records `acceptedSignoffRequired=true`, the embedded `acceptance-repeat-summary.json` has `vrmRsGitDirty=false` and the current clean `git rev-parse HEAD`, and the copied `acceptance-signoff.md` records matching vrm-rs/three-vrm source locks with numeric pass, accepted visual review, and complete signoff. The Just recipe then runs the existing one-environment strict intake smoke, so stale current-source bundles fail before being compared with returned runner evidence.
- Added `tools/render-parity/generate-acceptance-handoff.rs` and `just render-parity-acceptance-handoff` so the remaining multi-GPU/driver evidence task has a source-locked runner package. The handoff writes external-only Markdown/JSON with the current clean vrm-rs HEAD, origin URL, expected three-vrm commit, fixture-preparation command, strict runner command, returned bundle path, and final strict root-intake command. This makes the next external-runner request reproducible without embedding commit hashes in tracked docs.
- Added `tools/render-parity/audit-goal-readiness.rs` and `just render-parity-goal-readiness` as a machine-readable completion audit for the active render-parity goal. The audit checks clean current HEAD, optional `gh` public-repository visibility, current-source strict local bundle, source-locked runner handoff, and the final multi-environment strict summary. On the current single-runner state it should report local evidence and handoff as passing while keeping the goal incomplete until at least two distinct strict runner bundles are aggregated.
- Expanded that goal-readiness audit to cover the other explicit objective surfaces before the final render-parity environment gate: VRMA parity examples and local CI smokes, non-Bevy custom/wgpu/Ash adapter surfaces, concrete wgpu/Ash MToon materialization examples plus Ash shader handoff, and local external-fixture CI with no GitHub Actions workflows. The current audit now passes those objective checks and still reports only the final multi-environment strict acceptance summary as missing.
- Added `just render-parity-acceptance-stage-local-bundle` so the local strict accepted bundle can be copied into `.external-fixtures/render-parity-acceptance-returned/local/acceptance-bundle/` and validated through the returned-bundle root with `--min-environments 1`. This prepares the final intake parent while preserving the real completion requirement that at least one more distinct GPU/driver strict bundle must be added before `render-parity-acceptance-bundle-root-strict` can satisfy the goal.
- Tightened external runner handoff quality by adding the two-phase `just render-parity-acceptance-runner-capture` and `just render-parity-acceptance-runner-finalize-strict` recipes. The source-locked handoff generator now records both commands, and the goal-readiness audit requires them, so external runners can capture first, inspect visual-review pages/contact sheets/diff heatmaps, and only then write accepted signoff metadata and export a strict bundle. The older one-command strict runner remains available for deliberate local use.
- Added `tools/render-parity/preflight-acceptance-runner.rs` and `just render-parity-acceptance-runner-preflight` for external runner intake. The preflight checks Rust nightly, `just`, Node/npm, `glslangValidator`, exact repo HEAD, clean tracked worktree, pinned three-vrm checkout/build output, the six acceptance fixtures, and Ash shader compilation before a runner spends time on the long capture. The generated handoff and goal-readiness audit now require this preflight command.
- Added `tools/render-parity/generate-acceptance-runner-kit.rs` and `just render-parity-acceptance-runner-kit` so the source-locked handoff can be copied into a compact external-only runner kit with README, handoff JSON/Markdown, return-layout note, and intake command text. The kit deliberately excludes fixture binaries, generated rendered images, and golden files.
- External binary fixtures and generated golden files remain intentionally outside git under `.external-fixtures/`.
- P3 work should continue without committing official or third-party binary assets unless redistribution is explicitly reviewed for this MIT/Apache source repository.
